# Phase 1 UUID migration: frontend resource-ID audit

Audited 2026-10-16 (Asia/Kolkata), repository `feroz-hub/sbom`, branch `feat/native-user-management`, HEAD `2f48ff835d9e692fc84b31aaa951aad2495888ac`.

**Audit only.** This report describes the current working tree, including pre-existing user edits. Only this document was added. No frontend/application code, schema, data, dependencies, Git branch/ref or existing audit was changed. No app startup, requests, browser test, build or migration was run.

## Scope, method and severity

Inspected all 609 TypeScript/TSX files in `frontend/src`: 404 non-test source files and 205 test/support files. Parsed non-test sources with the repository TypeScript parser without executing them; zero syntax diagnostics. Also searched frontend E2E/configuration TypeScript and reviewed relevant tests. React pages/components, hooks, shared types, direct fetches, API helpers, BFF routes, query keys, navigation, dropdowns and browser persistence are included.

The source appendices retain 668 explicit numeric ID-like declaration occurrences (including nested/repeated declarations and number/string unions), 194 numeric conversion/validator calls with dispositions, 333 API/dashboard URL literal or template occurrences, and 143 navigation/query candidate source lines. These are source occurrences, not unique IDs, runtime routes or vulnerabilities. The name-based declaration scan is supplemented by reviewed aliases, lookup FKs, collections and implicit string-to-number boundaries; it is not a whole-program TypeScript dataflow proof.

FastAPI links below use the mounted registration catalog from [docs/uuid-migration/phase1/05-api-exposure.md:2702](/Users/ferozebasha/sbom/docs/uuid-migration/phase1/05-api-exposure.md:2702). Exact URL matches are source-path matches; direct request methods are extracted where explicit (default GET for direct request helpers); a template alone does not prove an HTTP method or reachable dispatch. The API appendix therefore lists matching backend registrations, retaining aliases and marking unresolved/dynamic expressions. Family links identify the relevant backend boundary, not a claim that every type is sent on every listed endpoint.

Severity is **UUID migration compatibility impact**, conditional on replacing the corresponding integer IDs: **High** = demonstrated coercion/rejection or lost scope/reconciliation; **Medium** = numeric wire/type/state/persistence/ordering assumptions requiring coordinated migration; **Low** = representation already string-capable but surrounding consumers need checking; **Info/excluded** = quantities, domain identifiers or non-resource state. No current exploit or security vulnerability is asserted.

## Identifier distinctions and already-string boundaries

- CVE/GHSA/PYSEC identifiers, bom-ref/SPDX, PURL/CPE, provider model names/keys, canonical component/family keys, external user/tenant subjects, role/permission codes and report cache hashes remain domain or external identifiers. They are not local integer database surrogate IDs merely because they contain digits.
- VEX assignee values are opaque strings such as `membership:<TenantUser.id>`; the local membership ID embedded in that namespace still matters. The UI preserves the complete string: [frontend/src/components/vex/AssigneeCombobox.tsx:30](/Users/ferozebasha/sbom/frontend/src/components/vex/AssigneeCombobox.tsx:30); [frontend/src/components/vex/VexInvestigationPanel.tsx:41](/Users/ferozebasha/sbom/frontend/src/components/vex/VexInvestigationPanel.tsx:41); [app/services/vex/authorization.py:24](/Users/ferozebasha/sbom/app/services/vex/authorization.py:24).
- Session/workspace, repair-job and AI batch IDs already use UUID strings. Mixed legacy `number | string` workspace/session declarations are compatibility debt, not evidence of an integer-only current DB PK: [frontend/src/lib/repairWorkspace.ts:16](/Users/ferozebasha/sbom/frontend/src/lib/repairWorkspace.ts:16); [frontend/src/lib/api.ts:2501](/Users/ferozebasha/sbom/frontend/src/lib/api.ts:2501); [frontend/src/components/ai-fixes/GlobalAiBatchProgress/AiBatchProgressContext.ts:14](/Users/ferozebasha/sbom/frontend/src/components/ai-fixes/GlobalAiBatchProgress/AiBatchProgressContext.ts:14).
- `sbom_type` is a numeric FK to an SBOMType lookup record and is included even without an `_id` suffix. `project_status` is a numeric status enum and is excluded: [frontend/src/types/index.ts:111](/Users/ferozebasha/sbom/frontend/src/types/index.ts:111); [frontend/src/types/index.ts:5](/Users/ferozebasha/sbom/frontend/src/types/index.ts:5).
- `row_version`, `expected_version`, `role_assignment_version`, policy `version`, `schema_version`, lifecycle revision, timestamps, size/line/stage numbers, score/severity ranks, money/budgets, retries, page/page_size/offset/limit and counts are numeric non-IDs. The backend keyset `cursor` is a record ID, but no frontend `X-Next-Cursor`/cursor request consumer was found; this frontend uses page/page_size.
- API URL interpolation, `String(id)`, URLSearchParams, React keys and HTML option values generally can carry UUID text. Numeric TypeScript annotations do not coerce at runtime. High ratings require a separate runtime gate or conversion, documented above.

## Explicit numeric resource-ID declaration inventory

Every row gives declaration context, field/parameter, exact type and source. Nested declarations on one line can repeat a field; context is the enclosing named declaration, not a complete serialized JSON path. Family = cross-reference above. ## API URL construction and backend registration matrix

Literal/template source occurrences are retained, including helpers and no-ID endpoints for coverage. Frontend method is extracted for direct request/fetch/download calls; helper/context marks expressions needing caller resolution. The backend column lists registered methods for the matched path, not an assertion that the frontend sends every listed method. Dynamic AI/lifecycle/policy base helpers and explicit scope/action branches expand to both tenant and platform aliases. Query values and JSON bodies are covered in findings and declarations. Medium on an ID interpolation is a coordinated contract dependency; the string interpolation itself is UUID-capable.

## Limits and preservation verification

This is a source audit, not a UUID compatibility test. No deployed frontend, API responses, browser storage contents, tenant switching or report downloads were exercised. Dynamic dictionary/provider payloads, arbitrary search text and externally supplied historical links may hold further identifiers; their complete runtime content cannot be established statically. No UUID implementation or acceptance claim follows from this inventory.

Branch/HEAD were rechecked before completion. The pre-existing nine tracked frontend modifications and untracked TenantMembersTable test were preserved byte-for-byte; audits 01–05 were preserved. Source references use this working-tree snapshot, so later source edits can move line numbers. Only `docs/uuid-migration/phase1/06-frontend.md` was written in the repository.
